Loser's effective force =13. Half =6.5. Need to lose ~6.5 attack from attacker.

Defender: spearmen 4 => most numerous = spearman. Archer counters spearman (since archer counters spearman). So attacker gets counter bonus: multiplies WHOLE army's force by 1.5.

Thus effective attack = 11 * 1.5 = 16.5.

Defender effective defense = 10 (no bonus for attacker? Defender gets location bonus already included). So attack 16.5 > defense 10 => attacker wins.

Loser's effective force = defender's effective defense =10. Half =5. Attacker loses troops worth 5 attack.

Now we need to compute losses from attacker army (before multiplier? The rule: "The WINNER loses troops worth HALF of the loser's effective force, spread over its troop types: that amount does not depend on the winner's size, so a bigger winning army loses a smaller share of itself." So we lose troops worth 5 attack from our attacking army (the one that fought). We need to subtract 5 attack from our army composition (1 spear,3 archer,1 knight).
